Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The rna variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 422 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 825 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on rna structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 422. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 825. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in rna theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 164 and the invariant I(A,B) = 160 mod 5.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the rna space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 164 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 160. QED.
